Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a magnetometry algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 345. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 900/2.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the magnetometry space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 345 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 900. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the magnetometry object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 990. The moduli space has dimension at most 801 with 5 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on magnetometry structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 990. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 801. QED.
